**Key Financial Highlights & Trends:**
*   **Top Line Growth:** Total Income grew robustly by **46% YoY** in H1 FY26, driven primarily by a 47% surge in Interest Income.
*   **Profitability:** Profit After Tax (PAT) expanded significantly by **74% YoY**, outpacing revenue growth, indicating improved operating leverage.
*   **Asset Quality / Provisioning:** Impairment provisions (provisions for NPAs) decreased by 21% YoY to ₹6,260 million, suggesting improved asset quality or relaxed provisioning requirements compared to H1 FY25.
*   **Expense Efficiency:** Total Expenses grew at 32% YoY against revenue growth of 46%, contributing to profit expansion. However, Finance Costs rose 45%, correlating with the growth in interest income (likely due to a larger loan book requiring more funding).
*   **Quarterly Momentum:** Q2 FY26 showed strong sequential growth (QoQ): Total Income up 13% and PAT up 22%.
*   **EPS:** Basic EPS nearly doubled from ₹60.67 (H1 FY25) to ₹110.52 (H1 FY26), an 82% increase.

# DIGITAL INITIATIVES
**Topic:** Digital convenience for new gen & Millennium Customer acquisition

**Direct Credit Facility**
*   Mechanism: Loan proceeds credited directly to bank account via NEFT/RTGS/IMPS mode of bank transfer.
*   Benefit: Instant credit to customer bank account and reduced cash handling at branch.

**PoS Terminals**
*   Enables swiping of Debit cards issued by banks.

**Loan Repayment Options**
Digital channels enabled for repayments include:
*   UPI (Powered by NPCI)
*   BBPS (Bharat Bill Payment System)
*   Google Pay
*   PhonePe
*   eCollection - Virtual A/C Remittance
*   UPI AutoPay and eMandate for EMI collections

**New Initiatives & Performance Metrics**
*   **Unsecured Cross-sell Vertical:** End-to-end (E2E) digital journey contributed **94% (31,500 cases)** of the total disbursals.
*   **WhatsApp Channel:** Enhanced to allow customers to check loan offers, apply for new loans, and download the iMuthoot app.
*   **DIY Processes:** Customers have the option to easily top up loans using simplified Do-It-Yourself processes.
*   **Payment Link Generation:** BBPS enabled payment link generation through SMS or WhatsApp.
*   **Payment Gateway:** Razorpay added to the iMuthoot Mobile App.
*   **Bill Payments:** Bill payments via gold loan enabled on iMuthoot.
*   **Top-Up Feature:** "Money on Call" feature introduced for customers to avail TopUp while on call with staff.

**Topic: Digital Initiatives – iMuthoot Mobile App**

**User Acquisition & Penetration**
*   **Launch Date:** November 2016
*   **Total Downloads:** Approximately 19.6 Million
*   **Registered Users:** 4.9 Million

**Operational Performance & Efficiency**
*   **Digital Repayment Adoption:** Gold loan interest repayments made through the iMuthoot app contributed to **33% of the total repayment volume**.
*   **Growth Trend:** Interest repayments via the app showed a **215% Year-over-Year (YoY) growth**.
*   **Transaction Volume:** Total transactions recorded at **7.8 Million** (including Empay & iMuthoot).

**Product Capabilities & Strategic Diversification**
*   **Core Functionality:** Simplified registration and login process; Online Gold Loan (OGL) facility available for withdrawing loan amounts, renewals, and payments.
*   **Cross-Selling Strategy:** Introduction of lead creation channels for additional product lines:
    *   Personal loans
    *   Business loans
    *   Home loans
    *   Loans against property

# IMPAIRMENT OF LOAN ASSETS & BAD DEBTS ANALYSIS

### Stage III Loans Assets and ECL Provision
*(Asset Quality and Provisioning Status in ₹ millions)*

| Metric | Sep-25 | Jun-25 | Mar-25 | Sep-24 |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **Stage I Loan Assets** | 1,287,935 | 1,158,809 | 1,044,433 | 851,124 |
| **Stage II Loan Assets** | 5,340 | 10,556 | 5,042 | 12,034 |
| **Stage III Loan Assets** | **29,774** | **30,945** | **37,004** | **38,807** |
| % Stage III Assets on Gross Loan Assets | **2.25%** | 2.58 | 3.41 | 4.30 |
| **ECL Provision on Gross Loan Assets** | **15,986** | **15,647** | **15,731** | **13,373** |
| ECL Provision as % of Gross Loan Assets | **1.21%** | 1.30 | 1.45 | 1.48 |
| Excess Provision outstanding in books | 2,954 | 2,954 | 2,954 | 2,954 |

**Key Observations:**
*   **Asset Quality Improvement:** Gross NPA (Stage III Assets) has improved significantly, declining from **4.30%** of Gross Loan Assets in Sep-24 to **2.25%** in Sep-25. In absolute terms, Stage III assets dropped from ₹38,807 mn to ₹29,774 mn over this period.
*   **Provision Coverage:** The ECL provision coverage ratio (as % of gross loan assets) has tightened from 1.48% to 1.21%.
*   **Hidden Reserves:** The company maintains a consistent **Excess Provision of ₹2,954 mn**, which acts as a hidden reserve available to smooth out future earnings or absorb shocks.

### Bad Debts / Write-offs Analysis
*(Realized Credit Costs in ₹ millions)*

| Metric | Q2 FY 2026 | Q1 FY 2026 | Q2 FY 2025 | H1 FY 2026 | H1 FY 2025 | FY 2025 |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **Bad Debts Written Off** | **776** | 495 | 160 | **1,271** | 288 | 1,269 |
| **% of Bad Debts written off to Gross Loan Assets** | **0.06%** | 0.04 | 0.02 | **0.10%** | 0.03 | 0.12% |

**Key Observations:**
*   **Write-off Spike:** There was a significant increase in bad debt write-offs in Q2 FY 2026 (**₹776 mn**) compared to Q2 FY 2025 (**₹160 mn**).
*   **Half-Year Trend:** Total write-offs for H1 FY 2026 were ₹1,271 mn, nearly matching the full year FY 2025 total of ₹1,269 mn.
*   **Cost Ratio:** While absolute write-offs rose in Q2, the cost relative to gross assets remains low at **0.06%**, though this is a quarter-on-quarter increase from 0.04%.

This slide presents historical and quarterly Return on Average Equity (ROAE) figures for Muthoot Finance, indicating profitability relative to shareholder equity. All starred figures denote calculations under IND-AS.

| Financial Year | ROE (%) |

| FY2024* | 17.86 |
| FY2025* | 19.73 |

**Key Observations from Data:**
*   **Loan Book Contraction:** Gross Loan AUM has declined significantly from ₹96,253m in H1 FY 2025 to ₹77,145m in H1 FY 2026.
*   **Profitability Swing:** The company shifted to a net loss in H1 FY 2026 with a PAT of (₹1,596)m compared to a profit of ₹1,424m in the prior year period. This is driven by expenses (₹10,539m) exceeding revenue (₹8,405m).
*   **Asset Quality Deterioration:** Stage III (Non-performing) Loan Assets increased to 3,353m in H1 FY 2026 from 2,981m YoY. Consequently, the percentage of Stage III assets on Gross Loan Assets rose to 4.58% from 3.51%.
*   **Balance Sheet Shrinking:** Total Assets have decreased from ₹91,102m in H1 FY 2025 to ₹79,752m in H1 FY 2026. Shareholder funds also eroded from ₹18,670m to ₹16,116m over the same period.
*   **Branch Expansion:** Network expansion slowed/contracted relative to the previous year; branches grew only marginally (1,111 to 1,287) while total headcount saw a slight dip (12,075 to 12,169) despite branch addition.

**Corporate Structure & Business Model**
*   **Subsidiary Status:** Muthoot Money Ltd (MML) is a wholly owned subsidiary of Muthoot Finance Ltd (became subsidiary in October 2018).

*   **Past Strategy:** Initially formed to lend for commercial vehicles and equipment.

**Key Financial Metric**
*   **Total Loan Portfolio:** Rs. 63,933 million (as of 30th September, 2025).

**Credit Profile**
*   **Debt Rating:** CRISIL assigned a Long Term Debt Rating of **CRISIL AA/Stable** for its bank limits.
